## Original PR description

## Background

This PR merges `main` into `dev` for the 100-commit slice `850bf4db..0156c213`. These are the oldest 100 commits of `dev..main`; commit 101 (`91d774de`) is excluded. The dev base is `ae76c60e`.

## What changed

- Merge `b6810054`, with parents dev `ae76c60e` and main `0156c213`.
- Squashed repair `73c827d0` (`fix: post-CI corrections`), 14 files, +98/-75:
  - Adapts `tensor_parallel/random.py` and MoE `router.py`/`token_dispatcher.py` to API changes from main. `random.py` keeps dev's #8390 port of #8334.
  - Adjusts 8 unit tests and removes duplicate entries from the gb200 unit-test recipe.
  - `examples/mimo_bagel/train.py` now reads `get_train_state().iteration`, because #7932 no longer sets `args.iteration` (review 5609274485).
  - `pyproject.toml` adds the `megatron.core.allocator` `csrc/*.cpp` package-data entry from #7494.
- Commit graph: 100 original main commits + 1 merge + 1 repair = 102.
- `Python lines: +40287 / -6292 across 410 files`

## Details

- CODEOWNERS, `uv.lock` and `Dockerfile.ci.dev` are identical to dev. `pyproject.toml` differs from dev only by the #7494 entry.
- The advisory flagged 4387 dev lines as possibly removed:
  - 4268 exist at the merge-base, so they are main-side edits.
  - 11 are whitespace-only.
  - 108 lines in 37 files trace to main commits in the slice (#8272, #7932, #7758 and others) or to the intended mimo_bagel fix.
  - Nothing was removed by accident, and nothing was restored.
- Remerge-diff covers 146 files: `git show --remerge-diff b6810054`.
- The MoE megakernel × `moe_virtual_expert_load_balance` question stays deferred to the MoE owners, with no code change.

## Strict review: nightly sync main → dev (2026-10-20)

**Findings:** 0 CRITICAL · 0 IMPORTANT · 1 SUGGESTION

**Overall risk:** low for the parts I checked, which include all of the repair commit's source changes. The rest of this 981-file sync was not checked, so I am leaving a comment rather than an approval.

### Finding
- **[SUGGESTION Simplification]** The repair commit added a `precomputed_indices` parameter to `InferenceTopKRouter._compiled_topk_routing` in [router.py](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/blob/0b27aa778b79dba3ea431ee7a6188c658a9ae623/megatron/core/transformer/moe/router.py#L1440). Nothing ever passes it a value. The only caller passes `precomputed_indices` only when it is set, and in exactly that case it uses the uncompiled `topk_routing_with_score_function` instead. The base code already had this caller unchanged, so the parameter does not fix a mismatch. Details are in the inline comment.

### What I checked and found no issue with
- **`MHCCheckpointManager` now subclasses `CheckpointWithoutOutputManager`** ([random.py](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/blob/0b27aa778b79dba3ea431ee7a6188c658a9ae623/megatron/core/tensor_parallel/random.py)). This matches the internals from main #8334. The RNG helpers it relies on (`_get_all_rng_states`, `_set_all_rng_states`, `_fork_rng`) exist and are also used by `RecomputeSegment` in [model_chunk_schedule_plan.py](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/blob/0b27aa778b79dba3ea431ee7a6188c658a9ae623/megatron/core/models/common/model_chunk_schedule_plan.py).
- **`_HybridEPManager.setup_metadata`** ([token_dispatcher.py](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/blob/0b27aa778b79dba3ea431ee7a6188c658a9ae623/megatron/core/transformer/moe/token_dispatcher.py#L1230-L1351)). The token budget is handled correctly in all three cases:
  - The rank-capacity branch sets the budget.
  - The branch with no capacity setting resets `num_permuted_tokens` to `None`.
  - drop-and-pad still overrides it with the capacity value, and combine keeps the static budgets.
- **Startup ownership after main #8272.** [`initialize_megatron`](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/blob/0b27aa778b79dba3ea431ee7a6188c658a9ae623/megatron/training/initialize.py) now calls `initialize_runtime_services`, which resolves the tokenizer vocab size. That service setup is not safe to run twice. Every changed entry point (BERT, MIMO, inference examples, detxoify, and the dev-only [multimodal_dev](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/blob/0b27aa778b79dba3ea431ee7a6188c658a9ae623/examples/multimodal_dev/pretrain_multimodal.py)) correctly dropped its own explicit call. `pretrain(..., *, build_tokenizer=...)` exists ([training.py](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/blob/0b27aa778b79dba3ea431ee7a6188c658a9ae623/megatron/training/training.py#L2810-L2828)), so `pretrain_mimo`'s `build_tokenizer=False` is valid.
- **MIMO changes.**
  - `DistributedInitConfig` has `align_grad_reduce` (default `True`) and `high_priority_stream_groups`, which [grad_sync.py](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/blob/0b27aa778b79dba3ea431ee7a6188c658a9ae623/examples/mimo/training/grad_sync.py) and [pretrain_mimo.py](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/blob/0b27aa778b79dba3ea431ee7a6188c658a9ae623/examples/mimo/pretrain_mimo.py) now read.
  - `get_data_lane_rank` reads `pg_collection.dp_gtp_remat`, which [topology.py](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/blob/0b27aa778b79dba3ea431ee7a6188c658a9ae623/examples/mimo/training/topology.py) populates for every grid.
- **Multimodal dataloader resume.** The new `(args, iteration)` signature is used consistently by `dataloader_provider` and its unit test.
- **CI.**
  - The new reusable [_unit-tests.yml](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/blob/0b27aa778b79dba3ea431ee7a6188c658a9ae623/.github/workflows/_unit-tests.yml) workflow runs the full bucket on GPU unless selective mode is `enforce`.
  - The coverage gate requires an empty-selection plan for every bucket.
  - Removing A100 from the GitLab stages is consistent across configure, run, cleanup, notify and publish.
  - The gb200 unit-test recipe now has a single `test_gated_delta_net.py` bucket.
- **Router quantile balancing.** The router implements both the `micro_batch` and `global_batch` estimation scopes, so having the virtual-expert tests select `micro_batch` only changes the tests.

- FDecaYed · APPROVED · 2026-10-22T04:29:39Z · [source](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/pull/8402#pullrequestreview-5609274485)

Approving the sync at `0b27aa77`. I re-reviewed the conflict resolutions and the repair commit, and audited dropped changes in both directions. I found no blocking merge errors. Two small merge-introduced issues should ideally be fixed before merge, or in a quick follow-up PR:

1. **`examples/mimo_bagel` still reads `args.curr_iteration` / `args.iteration`** ([L351](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/blob/0b27aa778b79dba3ea431ee7a6188c658a9ae623/examples/mimo_bagel/train.py#L351), [L583](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/blob/0b27aa778b79dba3ea431ee7a6188c658a9ae623/examples/mimo_bagel/train.py#L583)). Main #7932 stopped setting these on `args`, and this dev-only example wasn't updated. With PP>1 the data seed is now the same every step, so the numpy/random draws repeat each iteration. Fix: use `get_train_state().iteration`.

2. **Main #7494's package-data entry is missing from `pyproject.toml`.** Dev's `pyproject.toml` was kept, so `[tool.setuptools.package-data] "megatron.core.allocator" = ["csrc/*.cpp"]` didn't come over. Wheel and sdist installs won't ship `vmm_symm_allocator.cpp`, so the VMM allocator build fails there. Source-tree and editable CI runs are unaffected.

One combination for the MoE owners to confirm: dev's MOK megakernel (`moe_megakernel_backend="mok"`) and main's `moe_virtual_expert_load_balance` meet for the first time in this sync, and neither validation rejects the other. The megakernel path returns early ([L881](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/blob/0b27aa778b79dba3ea431ee7a6188c658a9ae623/megatron/core/transformer/moe/moe_layer.py#L881)), so the virtual-expert wrap/finalize may not pair up. If the combination isn't supported, a mutual-exclusion check would be the safe option.

Agree with keeping `precomputed_indices`: main #7310's test calls it directly. MBridge tests didn't run because the label is missing.

### inline_comments

- svcnvidia-nemo-ci · comment · 2026-10-21T16:32:18Z · [source](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/pull/8402#discussion_r4339310383)

File: `megatron/core/transformer/moe/router.py` · line 1440

**[SUGGESTION Simplification]** Strict mode checks for unused arguments: the new `precomputed_indices=None` parameter of `InferenceTopKRouter._compiled_topk_routing` is never given a value.

**Problem:** The only caller is `_forward` ([L1476-L1500](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/blob/0b27aa778b79dba3ea431ee7a6188c658a9ae623/megatron/core/transformer/moe/router.py#L1476-L1500)). It adds `precomputed_indices` to `routing_kwargs` only when `self.qb_beta is not None`. In exactly that case it picks the uncompiled `topk_routing_with_score_function` (`routing = topk_routing_with_score_function if precomputed_indices is not None or is_batch_invariant_mode_enabled() else self._compiled_topk_routing`). So when `_compiled_topk_routing` runs, `routing_kwargs == {}` and `precomputed_indices` is always `None`.

The base caller is identical ([base L1454-L1478](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/blob/ff4fbc9f3a0e0f390d2acda51c641b3f2bc495d4/megatron/core/transformer/moe/router.py#L1454-L1478)) and never passed the argument to the compiled function. The commit message's reason ("matching the base router call site") therefore does not correspond to an actual mismatch. The dead parameter also suggests the quantile-balancing path is compiled when it is not.

**Fix (smallest change):** remove the parameter and the forwarded keyword so the compiled helper matches its real contract.
```diff
         router_replay,
         dense_output,
-        precomputed_indices=None,
     ):
         return topk_routing_with_score_function(
             ...
             dense_output=dense_output,
-            precomputed_indices=precomputed_indices,
         )
```
If you actually want quantile-balanced (`qb_beta`) inference routing to run through `torch.compile`, make that change on purpose in `_forward`'s routing choice instead of leaving a parameter that is never used. That alternative changes behaviour and compilation (an optional tensor argument can trigger recompiles), so it needs its own validation.

**Behaviour to keep:**
1. With `qb_beta` set, `_forward` keeps calling the uncompiled `topk_routing_with_score_function(..., precomputed_indices=...)`.
2. Without `qb_beta` and outside batch-invariant mode, `_forward` keeps calling `_compiled_topk_routing` with the same 11 arguments it uses today.
3. In batch-invariant mode, the uncompiled path stays in use.
4. Hash-layer routing is unaffected.

**How to verify:**
- *Static check:* after the change, confirm that nothing passes `precomputed_indices` to `_compiled_topk_routing`. I found no tests that reference `_compiled_topk_routing`.
- *Tests to run (I could not run them):* `tests/unit_tests/transformer/moe/test_routers.py` and `tests/unit_tests/inference/test_moe_dispatching_and_routing.py`. They should still pass, in particular the inference-router and batch-invariant training/inference parity cases (`assert_close(..., atol=0, rtol=0)`).
